- **buy-x402**: Buy from any x402-gated endpoint. Start with `go &lt;url&gt;` — it probes, detects the offer type, and runs the right flow. Expert flows underneath: `pay` for one-shot HTTP services (single authorization, no sidecar), `pay-agent` for one-shot streaming agent calls, and `buy` for long-running paid inference (pre-authorized batch via PurchaseRequest, exposed as `paid/&lt;remote-model&gt;`). Supports USDC (EIP-3009) and OBOL (Permit2). Zero signer access at runtime — spending is capped by design and nothing moves on-chain until a voucher is spent. ([ObolNetwork/obol-stack](https://github.com/ObolNetwork/obol-stack/tree/HEAD/internal/embed/skills/buy-x402) · ★11)

- **bwa-mem2-dna-aligner**: Fast short-read DNA aligner for WGS/WES/ChIP-seq. 2× faster BWA-MEM successor; outputs SAM/BAM with read group headers for GATK. Primary plus supplementary records for chimeric reads. Use STAR for RNA-seq splice-aware alignment; Bowtie2 is a comparable alternative. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/alignment/bwa-mem2-dna-aligner) · ★365 · 4 copies)
- **bwa-mem2-dna-aligner**: 当需要把 Illumina 短读 DNA（50–250 bp，WGS/WES/ChIP-seq/ATAC-seq）比对到参考基因组、生成带 @RG 读组头的排序 BAM 供 GATK/Picard 变异检测或 peak calling 时使用；用 bwa-mem2 index 建索引、bwa-mem2 mem 比对并管道接 samtools sort/index、再 markdup 去重，产出 sorted/markdup BAM 与 flagstat 质控；不适用于跨剪接位点的 RNA-seq（用 star-rnaseq-aligner）、&lt;50bp 极短读（用 BWA-backtrack）、长读（用 minimap2）。触发词：bwa-mem2、DNA 比对、短读、WGS、WES、ChIP-seq、ATAC-seq、@RG 读组、BAM、samtools sort、markdup、参考基因组比对 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/bwa-mem2-dna-aligner) · ★3)

- **bwh-accessibility**: Run human-steered accessibility improvement rounds for bounded journeys; not a compliance certification or a one-defect fix. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-accessibility))
- **bwh-adopt**: Install or update the BWH Agent Toolkit in a project, select a catalog profile, preserve local project instructions and customisations, and record the exact pinned installation. Use when asked to adopt, install, sync, upgrade, update, or change a BWH toolkit profile. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-adopt))
- **bwh-agent-review**: Independently review completed implementation against the authorised request or approved specification, project guardrails, validation, and acceptance criteria. Use before accepting delivered work; use bwh-pr-review for a complete PR or branch review before merge. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-agent-review))
- **bwh-api-usability**: Improve an API's usability for its consumers through bounded implementation and iterative review of contracts, examples, errors, and common tasks. Use for API usability rounds; exclude standalone audits, bug-only fixes, documentation-only edits, and unrequested breaking changes. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-api-usability))
- **bwh-archive-change**: Archive an accepted delivered change and its evidence-linked temporary documentation into a verified, manifest-backed bundle. Use only when the human explicitly requests archival or closure of accepted change documentation. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-archive-change))
- **bwh-ask**: Answer a question about the repository, an idea, or the workflow state without creating or advancing any artifact. Use before ideation, mid-spec, mid-development, or whenever a quick answer is needed without triggering a workflow stage. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-ask))
- **bwh-blast-radius**: Review what a proposed or implemented code change could break beyond its diff, then prove the smallest important safety claims with repository evidence or real execution. Use when the user asks for a blast-radius review, asks what a change could break, or wants a focused pre-merge risk check. Do not use for broad architecture review or completed-implementation acceptance review. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-blast-radius))
- **bwh-create-verification**: Create a project-local verify-&lt;app&gt; skill that launches, checks, drives, observes, and safely cleans up the project's primary user-facing system. Use when a repository lacks a repeatable user-level verification workflow or the user asks to create a verification or control skill for an app. Do not use merely to run an existing verification skill. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-create-verification))
- **bwh-database-performance**: Run human-steered database performance improvement rounds; not a general database review or a one-query repair. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-database-performance))
- **bwh-developer-experience**: Improve setup, build, test, and debugging workflows through human-steered implementation and review rounds. Exclude standalone audits, ordinary bug fixes, documentation-only edits, and global tooling changes. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-developer-experience))
- **bwh-development**: Implement authorised scoped requests or approved planned tasks with focused changes, project validation, and independent review. Use for requested fixes, features, and implementation work. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-development))
- **bwh-diagnose-bugs**: Diagnose hard bugs and performance regressions through a repeatable feedback loop, minimized reproduction, falsifiable hypotheses, and targeted evidence. Use when the user asks to diagnose or debug broken, failing, throwing, flaky, or slow behavior. If the user asks only for diagnosis, report the cause and evidence without implementing a fix. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-diagnose-bugs))
- **bwh-documentation-usability**: Improve whether readers can use published project documentation to complete real tasks through bounded edits and iterative review. Use for documentation-usability rounds; exclude standalone audits, bug fixes, agent-instruction changes, and product-code work. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-documentation-usability))
- **bwh-grill**: Run an exhaustive, decision-tree interview about a plan, design, or idea. Trigger only when the user explicitly asks to be grilled, interrogated, exhaustively questioned, or stress-tested through questions. Do not trigger for ordinary clarification, ideation, specification, review, or requests to implement work. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-grill))
- **bwh-ideate**: Turn an early product or engineering idea into a bounded discovery brief with the problem, desired outcome, actors, constraints, options, and questions needed before writing a specification. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-ideate))
- **bwh-maintainability**: Improve maintainability through a bounded implementation round grounded in representative change tasks and observed repository costs. Use for iterative maintainability work; exclude standalone architecture audits, bug-only fixes, documentation-only edits, and speculative rewrites. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-maintainability))
- **bwh-model-selector**: Select models and reasoning effort for delegated work whenever an agent plans or executes a task, including specification, development, investigation, and review. Prefer capable lower-tier subagents, require permission for higher-tier models, and warn when model guidance is stale. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-model-selector))
- **bwh-pr-review**: Independently review a complete pull request or branch before merge and return a revision-bound verdict. Use for local pre-merge review, release PRs and re-review after changes; not implementing review feedback or the specification acceptance lifecycle. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-pr-review))
- **bwh-prototype**: Build an isolated throwaway prototype to answer one product or engineering decision about logic, state, data shape, layout, hierarchy, or interaction. Use when evidence from a runnable experiment is needed before specification or development. Do not use to implement approved production work or treat prototype code as production-ready. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-prototype))
- **bwh-refine-spec**: Iteratively refine a draft specification and its development-readiness artifacts from human feedback or repository evidence until the spec is approved for agentic development. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-refine-spec))
- **bwh-reliability**: Run human-steered reliability improvement rounds on bounded systems; not a broad risk audit or a one-off incident fix. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-reliability))
- **bwh-retro**: Review how a delivered change was executed, propose durable improvements to the project's agent instructions or to the workflow toolkit, and record them where the project already captures actions. Use only when the human asks for a retro, a post-mortem, or what could be done better next time, which often follows archiving an accepted change. Do not use to review a work product for defects, which bwh-agent-review owns, or to audit a toolkit installation, which bwh-skills-audit owns. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-retro))
- **bwh-review-architecture**: Review a bounded subsystem for evidence-backed architecture improvements when maintenance, comprehension, testing, or change locality is costly. Use for a pre-spec structural survey and ranked refactor candidates. Do not use for post-implementation acceptance review owned by bwh-agent-review, and stop before interface design or implementation. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-review-architecture))
- **bwh-skills-audit**: Audit a BWH Agent Toolkit installation or skill collection by comparing its source catalog, installed lock, installed directories, and source revision. Use to report missing or unexpected skills, drift, local modifications, broken references, invalid frontmatter, inactive profile entries, usage evidence, or retirement candidates. Default to read-only reporting and never use this skill to install, remove, or update anything without explicit approval. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-skills-audit))
- **bwh-spec**: Turn a rough product or engineering idea into a bounded specification with development-readiness artifacts for human approval and downstream execution. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-spec))
- **bwh-technical-writing**: Write or revise human-facing technical documentation, including READMEs, tutorials, how-to guides, runbooks, reference pages, explanations, ADRs, pull request descriptions, commit messages, and edits to an existing specification. Use when accuracy, structure, terminology, and testable instructions matter. Do not use to create or refine a product or engineering specification; bwh-spec or bwh-refine-spec owns that work. Do not use for instructions whose primary reader is an agent; bwh-write-agent-instructions owns that work. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-technical-writing))
- **bwh-ux-polish**: Polish existing UI/UX journeys or pages through independent design reviews, scored evidence, implementation, and human-steered rounds. Use for iterative UI/UX polish with a reviewer team and a human checkpoint after every round. Do not use for a read-only audit, a throwaway layout prototype, or an ordinary UI fix without design rounds. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-ux-polish))
- **bwh-web-performance**: Run human-steered improvement rounds for web performance journeys; not a general site audit or a single-issue fix. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-web-performance))
- **bwh-write-agent-instructions**: Design, revise, or audit instructions whose primary reader is an agent, including skills, shared contracts, adapters, project agent instruction files, and documents reached through pointers. Use for trigger design, instruction hierarchy, context cost, completion criteria, pruning, and behavioral evals. Do not use for general technical documentation owned by bwh-technical-writing. ([simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow](https://github.com/simplybenuk/bwh-ai-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/bwh-write-agent-instructions))
- **bwoc-chat**: Open an interactive chat session with a BWOC agent using its manifest-driven backend/model. Wraps `bwoc chat`. Use for back-and-forth conversation rather than one-shot tasks. ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-codex](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-codex/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-chat))
- **bwoc-council**: Run formal multi-agent decisions and objective tracking on a BWOC workspace from Claude Code — open a council decision (propose), discuss, vote, and resolve by the plugin's voting model + quorum, plus list/track/report OKR key results. Use when a team needs a governed decision recorded (not just a chat) or wants to track objectives. Wraps the `bwoc` CLI; the live paths need a council/okr-kind plugin installed. ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-council))
- **bwoc-fleet**: Coordinate the BWOC agent fleet from Claude Code. - **bwoc-fleet**: Overview of how to coordinate the BWOC agent fleet from OpenAI Codex. Explains when to use each bwoc verb (list/status/send/run/chat/task/team/memory) and how they fit together. - **bwoc-fleet**: Coordinate the BWOC agent fleet from your AI coding agent. Use when the user wants to list/inspect BWOC agents, delegate or send work to a fleet member, run a headless task on an agent, manage Saṅgha teams or their shared task list, or read workspace deep-memory. Wraps the `bwoc` CLI (shell-out, no server). ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-vercel](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-vercel/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-fleet))
- **bwoc-health**: Diagnose the health of a BWOC workspace and its agent fleet from Claude Code. Use when the user wants to check fleet/workspace health, find a stuck or unhealthy agent, see which agents are actually running, read an agent's daemon log, inspect trust profiles, or triage before launching work. Read-only diagnostics that wrap the `bwoc` CLI (shell-out, no server). ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-health))
- **bwoc-knowledge**: Read and write a BWOC workspace's shared knowledge from Claude Code — deep-memory plus the dated document kinds (notes, retrospectives, research, and custom doc kinds). Use when the user wants to recall prior decisions, capture a note or retro after finishing work, file a research doc, or browse what the workspace already knows. Wraps the `bwoc` CLI (shell-out, no server). ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-knowledge))
- **bwoc-lifecycle**: Manage the lifecycle of BWOC agents from Claude Code — incarnate (new), pause/resume (stop/start), supervise the daemon, inspect the base-project binding (debase), and retire. Use when the user wants to add a new agent, pause or reactivate one, keep a daemon alive, or remove an agent from the workspace. Wraps the `bwoc` CLI (shell-out, no server); the mutating verbs follow the workspace's own gates. ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-lifecycle))
- **bwoc-list**: List BWOC agents registered in the workspace. Wraps `bwoc list`. Use to discover which agents exist, their backend, status, and pending inbox counts. ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-codex](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-codex/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-list))
- **bwoc-memory**: Read and search BWOC workspace-level memory (`.bwoc/memory/`) and Tier-2 deep memory. Wraps `bwoc memory`. Use to recall prior decisions, notes, and session context. ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-codex](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-codex/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-memory))
- **bwoc-messaging**: Read and send messages on a BWOC agent's inbox from Claude Code — inspect an agent's (or the whole fleet's) inbox, count or tail new envelopes, acknowledge/clear a backlog, append a message, and read daemon logs. Use when the user wants to see what work is queued for an agent, triage a backed-up inbox, or hand off a message. Wraps the `bwoc` CLI (shell-out, no server). ([bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude](https://github.com/bemindlabs/bwoc-plugin-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/bwoc-messaging))
